# Player-only guide UI. Only MMG_UI_page/MMG_UI_choices may be written.
# No gameplay hooks, decisions, events, random effects or logging.
scripted_gui = {
 MMG_UI_launcher = {
  context_type = player_context
  window_name = "MMG_UI_Launcher"
  # Mount on the full-screen in-game topbar, not the default main window.
  # This is an additive child; interface/topbar.gui is never overridden.
  parent_window_token = top_bar
  visible = { is_ai = no }
  ai_enabled = { always = no }
  effects = {
   MMG_UI_Open_click = {
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_page = 1 }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_choices = 8 }
   }
  }
 }
 MMG_UI_viewer = {
  context_type = player_context
  # Keep the panel on the same in-game layer as its launcher.
  parent_window_token = top_bar
  window_name = "MMG_UI_Viewer"
  dirty = MMG_UI_page
  visible = { is_ai = no has_variable = MMG_UI_page }
  ai_enabled = { always = no }
  properties = { MMG_UI_Picture = { image = "[MMG_UI_GetImage]" } }
  triggers = {
   MMG_UI_Option_1_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_OptionLabel_1_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_Option_2_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_OptionLabel_2_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_Option_3_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_OptionLabel_3_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_Option_4_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_OptionLabel_4_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_Option_5_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 5 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_OptionLabel_5_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 5 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_Option_6_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 6 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_OptionLabel_6_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 6 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_Option_7_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 7 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_OptionLabel_7_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 7 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_Option_8_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 8 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
   MMG_UI_OptionLabel_8_visible = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_choices value = 8 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
  }

   MMG_UI_Home_click = {
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_page = 1 }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_choices = 8 }

      clear_variable = MMG_UI_choices
   }

     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 1 compare = equals } }

      set_variable = { MMG_UI_choices = 8 }
    }
    else_if = {
     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 10100 compare = equals } }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_page = 10101 }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_choices = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 10101 compare = equals } }

      set_variable = { MMG_UI_choices = 8 }

    }
    else_if = {

    if = {
     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 1 compare = equals } }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_page = 10400 }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_choices = 8 }
    }
    else_if = {
     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 10100 compare = equals } }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_page = 10104 }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_choices = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 10106 compare = equals } }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_page = 30063 }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_choices = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 10300 compare = equals } }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_page = 10600 }
      set_variable = { MMG_UI_choices = 8 }
    }
    else_if = {
     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 10301 compare = equals } }
      clear_variable = MMG_UI_page
      clear_variable = MMG_UI_choices
    }
    else_if = {
     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 10302 compare = equals } }
      clear_variable = MMG_UI_page
      clear_variable = MMG_UI_choices
    }
    else_if = {
     limit = { check_variable = { var = MMG_UI_page value = 10303 compare = equals } }
